  x = simplex_project(normalize)
  best = x, bestf = score(x)
  τ = 0.1 maybe relative; topK = 512
  for iter in loop:
     c = fft_convolve(x)
     fmax = c.max()
     update best
     # weights for top K? Use softmax over top K? Or full with T = fmax*τ
     T = fmax*τ (if fmax>0)
     u = c - cmax
     w = exp(u/T); w/=sum
     # To reduce noise, set weights only top p where w>eps? Could topK=2048.

     # Backtracking: try step, if score improves accept else half few.
     # Anneal τ, step.
  return best

Spectral method for convolution (not autocorr): If c = x*x, then C(ω)=X(ω)^2. Given a desired c (say flat 1 over length N), choose X=sqrt(C). For real c, C may be complex. We can choose phase of X to make x real? Since C from real x has C(ω) with symmetry C(-ω)=C(ω) (not conjugate for convolution? X(-ω)^2 = X(ω)^2 because real: X(-)=X*, so square? C(-)=X(-)^2=X*², which is conjugate of X²? Yes C is Hermitian. DFT of real c is Hermitian. sqrt can choose phases such that X(-ω)=conj(X(ω)). Then x real. But nonnegative? Project. Iterate: set X=sqrt(DFT(c_current))? If c_current = x*x, DFT is X^2 exactly. If we modify c to target, update.

Algorithm "Rienstra / Griffin-Lim" for nonnegative sequences with given convolution:
Given c_target (maybe smoothed desired shape), find x≥0 sum=1 minimizing? Use:
1. Compute X = sqrt(DFT(c_target)) with consistent phase.
2. x = Re(ifft(X)); project nonnegative and normalize.
3. c = x*x; update c_target by combining target and c (e.g., c_target = c_target * (c/c_target)^α?); iterate.
This optimizes spectral domain and could explore global. Previous did spectral shaping of P=|A|² (autocorr), not A². We can implement convolution spectral projection.

Detailed algorithm for a given sequence a (nonnegative sum1) and n,N:

def improve_sequence(a, n, N, budget, rng, label):
  a = normalize_sum1(copy)
  best = a, best_score = score(a)
  it=0
  best_U = best_score  # target ratio v_max (approx score)
  gamma=0.75; damp=0.6; grad_step=1e-3; T_temp=0.1
  stall=0
  start=time.monotonic()
  while time < start+budget:
    c=conv(a)
    cur=c.max()
    sc=2*n*cur
    if sc < best_score - 1e-12: best=...
    # update target U slowly down toward 1.0: U=best_score - 0.02? But score not exactly vmax due n; use cur/mean.
    mean=1/(2*n-1)
    vmax=cur/mean
    # choose target U = max(1.0+0.02, vmax - 0.1*stall?) not.
    # spectral step
    for trial in [0.9,0.8,0.7,0.6,0.5]? maybe each iteration use one.
    a_new = spectral_flatten(a,N,n,alpha=alpha,damp=damp)
    sc_new=score(a_new)
    accept if sc_new<sc (or maybe w.r.t best) else try lower damp/alpha; else perturb.
    # gradient step
    if it % 10==...:
       a_new = gradient_step(a,N,n,tau=..., eta=...)
    # random restart if stall many.
  return best

Target generation from current c:
def make_compressed_target(c, score_vmax, U):
   mean=1.0/(2*n-1)
   v=c/mean
   m=max(v)
   if m <=1+1e-9: return None
   if U >= m*0.9999: return None
   a=(U-1)/(m-1)
   target = a*v + (1-a)
   # normalize mean exactly? due float
   target = target / target.mean()
   # ensure nonnegative
   return target

def coordinate_polish(a,n,budget,rng,n_top=64,n_factors=5, rounds=None):
  a=normalize_sum1; c=conv(a,N)
  best...
  start=time; while time:
    maximize? We try to reduce score.
    cur_score=2n*c.max()
    update
    # active lags top K (50)
    top_lags=np.argsort(c)[::-1][:K] maybe pick all tied? Sorting 16k each sweep okay.
    # contributions
    contrib=np.zeros(n)
    for k in top_lags:
       low=max(0,k-n+1); high=min(k,n-1)
       # vectorized over i range:
       ii=np.arange(low,high+1)
       jj=k-ii
       contrib[ii]+=2*a[ii]*a[jj]
    top_vars=np.argsort(contrib)[::-1][:n_top]
    improved=False
    factors = [0.3,0.5,0.7,0.85,0.95,1.05,1.15,1.3]
    for i in top_vars:

       old=a[i]
       if old < 1e-12: continue
       best_f=1.0; best_score=cur_score; best_cmax=c.max()
       # To avoid copy c for each factor huge, we can vectorize candidate delta array.

       maxes = cand.max(axis=1)
       Ss = 1 + deltas
       scores = 2*n*maxes/(Ss*Ss)
       idx=np.argmin(scores)
       chosen_f=factors[idx]
       if scores[idx] < best_score - 1e-12:

           # update a and c in place
           a[i]=old+δ
           c[i:i+Lslice] += 2*δ*a_old_vector? Wait a[:Lslice] must be old a before changing? The change formula uses old a[j] for j≠i and old a_i for diagonal? Now implement project_simplex robust. Use in-place? Top-level:
def project_simplex(v, S):
    n=len(v)
    if n==0: return v
    v=np.asarray(v,dtype=np.float64)
    if S==0: return np.zeros(n)
    w=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    css=np.cumsum(w)
    rho=np.arange(1,n+1)
    theta=(css-S)/rho
    valid=theta<=w
    if not valid.any():
        return np.zeros(n) ? If theta? Standard fallback: if no valid, rho=0 theta=v.mean? But v nonnegative? For projection to sum S, if all theta > w means S<0? Not happen if v nonneg and S>=0. Use mean.
    rho=int(np.max(np.where(valid)[0]))+1
    theta=(css[rho-1]-S)/rho
    return np.maximum(v-theta,0.0)
This normalizes? It projects to sum S. We then maybe if sum not exact due round, divide.
For S=1.

central_sequence:
def central_sequence(n):
    a=np.empty(n,float); a[0]=1; for k in range(1,n): a[k]=a[k-1]*(2*k-1)/(2*k)
    return a
This is float64 for k=8192 ~0.006. Sum ~102. Normalization fine. For large n loop 8192 trivial; for 16384 okay.

convolve_fft:
def convolve_fft(a,N=None):
    n=len(a)
    if N is None: N=1<<(2*n-2).bit_length() # L=2n-1
    A=fft(a,N)
    c=real(ifft(A*A))
    return c[:2*n-1]
Need A*A not conjugate. For real a, linear conv. Works. Because N≥2n-1 ensures no wrap. If N exactly 2n-1? next power2.

score_internal:
def score_internal(a,c=None):
    s=a.sum()
    if s <=1e-15: return inf
    if c is None: c=convolve_fft(a)
    m=float(c.max())
    n=len(a)
    return float(2.0*n*m/(s*s))
If a not normalized, okay.

make_compressed_target(c,n,U):
    L=2*n-1; mean=1.0/L
    v=c/mean
    m=float(v.max())
    if m <=1+1e-9: return None
    if U <=1: return None
    if U >= m: U=m*0.999 -? Ensure lower.
    a_c=(U-1)/(m-1)
    if a_c <=0 or a_c>=1: maybe skip
    target_v = a_c*v + (1-a_c)
    # ensure nonneg
    # Normalize mean to 1/L: target_actual = target_v / L? Since target_v mean1? a_c*1+(1-a_c)=1. target_v mean approx1. Multiply by mean: target=target_v*mean. Its sum L*mean=1. Good.
    target=target_v*mean
    # Clean tiny negatives
    target=np.maximum(target,0)
    # rescale sum to1: target /= target.sum() (but sum may <1 due clip)
    ss=target.sum()
    if ss>0: target/=ss
    return target

This target time sum1. Note max after clipping might not exactly U/L if sum rescale. Good.

def spectral_compress(a,n,N,budget,rng,label=''):
    start=time.monotonic(); deadline=start+budget
    a=normalize_sum1(a.copy())
    c=convolve_fft(a,N) maybe not need N? score_internal

    it=0; last_improve=start; stall=0
    # parameter schedule
    while time < deadline-2:
        it+=1
        cur=score_internal(a) # conv computed internally; could reuse c but after spectral new
        # update best
        # choose factor based on current m
        mean=1.0/(2*n-1)

        m=c.max()/mean
        # factor choices; lean smaller if improving, larger if stalling

        if best_score <= 1.505: small choices
        choices = [0.999,0.995,0.99,0.98,0.95,0.9]
        if stall_time > 60: weight large

        target=make_compressed_target(c,n,U)
        if target is None:
            # do small random perturbation
            a=project_simplex(a+rng.normal(0,0.001/np.sqrt(n),n),1)
            continue
        # try two damp values?
        accepted=False
        for damp in [0.35,0.6,0.85,0.4]:? Could be too many. Use one damp = 0.5 maybe with random plus noise if no improve.

        sc_new=score_internal(a_new)
        if sc_new < cur:

           a = project_simplex(best + rng.normal(0,0.005/√n,n),1)
    return best,best_score

def coordinate_polish(a,n,N,budget,rng,label=''):
    a=normalize_sum1(a.copy())
    L=2*n-1
    c=convolve_fft(a,N)
    cur=2.0*n*c.max()
    best=a.copy(); best_score=cur
    start=time.monotonic(); deadline=start+budget
    factors = [0.2,0.4,0.6,0.8,0.9,1.1,1.25,1.5] # maybe include 1.0 implicit? We can always consider 1.0 as baseline; no need in list? But if no candidate beats baseline, keep.
    top_lag_count=32; var_count=64
    rounds=0
    while time < deadline-1:
       # maybe recompute c every 5 rounds to correct
       if rounds %5 ==0:
          c=convolve_fft(a,N); cur=2*n*c.max(); if cur<best_score update
       else:
          cur=2*n*c.max()
       if cur < best_score: best...
       # If cur very close to lower maybe break? We can still.
       # top lags
       # use argsort maybe expensive; top 32 from c
       top_lags=np.argpartition(c, -top_lag_count)[-top_lag_count:]
       # contributions
       contrib=np.zeros(n)
       for k_int in top_lags:
          k=int(k_int)
          low=max(0,k-n+1); high=min(k,n-1)
          # vectorized
          ii=np.arange(low, high+1)
          jj=k-ii
          contrib[ii] += 2.0*a[ii]*a[jj]
       top_vars=np.argpartition(contrib, -var_count)[-var_count:]
       improved=False
       for i in top_vars:
          i=int(i); old=float(a[i])
          if old <= 1e-15: continue
          Lslice = min(n, 2*n-2 - i +1) # n
          # outside max
          if i>0:
             max_out_left = float(c[:i].max()) if i>0 else 0.0
          else:
             max_out_left = 0.0
          right_start = i + Lslice
          max_out_right = float(c[right_start:].max()) if right_start < L else 0.0
          max_out = max(max_out_left, max_out_right)
          old_vec = a[:Lslice].copy()
          deltas = old*(np.array(factors) - 1.0)
          # Candidate raw slice = c[i:right_start] + 2*deltas * old_vec
          base = c[i:right_start]
          cand = base[None,:] + (2.0*deltas[:,None]*old_vec[None,:])
          maxes = np.maximum(cand.max(axis=1), max_out)
          Ss = 1.0 + deltas
          # Avoid S<=0 since factors include 0.2 -> S=0.8>0
          scores = (2.0*n*maxes)/(Ss*Ss)
          idx=int(np.argmin(scores))
          if scores[idx] < cur - 1e-12:
              δ=deltas[idx]
              S=1.0+δ
              # update c: old c slice plus raw increment, then scale all by 1/S²
              c[i:right_start] += 2.0*δ*old_vec
              a[i] = old + δ
              # scale a and c
              scale = 1.0/S
              a *= scale
              c *= (scale*scale)
              cur = float(scores[idx])  # but after scaling c, max maybe not exactly due floating; okay
              improved=True
              break? Should we break after one coordinate? In coordinate descent, we update one variable and then contributions change; loop next i using stale contrib? We can break to recompute top variables? But one pass over top_vars before recomputing contributions may be okay if we don't break? The contributions stale and c changed; trying next i based on old contributions might still okay but less optimal.        if not improved:
          # random perturb M top variables
          if rng.random()<0.7 and var_count>0:
             idxs=top_vars[:min(8,var_count)]
             for idx in idxs:
                if idx>=0 and a[idx]>0:

def consider_candidate(arr, best_score, best_seq, tag, verbose=True):
    if arr is None: return best_score,best_seq
    arr=np.asarray(arr,dtype=float).ravel()
    if arr.size<2: return...
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(arr)): return
    arr=np.clip(arr,0,1000)
    s=arr.sum()
    if s<=0.01: return
    arr_n=arr/s
    sc=score_internal(arr_n)
    if sc < best_score:
        if verbose: print(...)
        return sc, arr_n.copy()
    return best_score,best_seq

if N%2==0:
    X_t_half = math.sqrt(abs(C[nhalf]))
    if X_ref[nhalf] < 0: X_t_half = -X_t_half

X_pos = X_t[1:nhalf].copy()
ref_pos = X_ref[1:nhalf]

X_pos[dots < 0] = -X_pos[dots < 0]
X_t[1:nhalf]=X_pos
X_t[nhalf+1:] = np.conj(X_pos[::-1])

Smooth_gradient_step top-level:
def smooth_gradient_step(a,c,N,n,tau,eta):
    n=len(a); L=2*n-1
    cur=float(c.max())
    if cur<=0: return a
    T=tau*cur
    if T<=1e-18: T=1e-18
    u=(c-cur)/T
    # exp underflow okay; values negative
    w=np.exp(u)
    ws=w.sum()
    if ws<=0: return a
    w /= ws
    w_pad=np.zeros(N); w_pad[:L]=w
    a_pad=np.zeros(N); a_pad[:n]=a
    grad=np.real(ifft(fft(w_pad)*fft(a_pad)))[:n]
    grad *= 2.0
    grad -= grad.mean()
    gn=float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
    if gn <=1e-15: return a
    x=a - (eta/gn)*grad  # step size eta in L2
    x=project_simplex(x,1.0)
    return x
Since `eta` variable is step length in component L2; choose 0.02-0.1. If eta=0.05, total change ~0.05. For n=8192, large. Maybe too large. Use eta=0.02. Could try [0.05,0.02,0.01]. We'll just use 0.02.

def consider_candidate(arr, best_score, best_seq, tag, verbose=True):
    if arr is None:
        return best_score, best_seq
    arr=np.asarray(arr,dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if arr.size < 8:
        return best_score,best_seq
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(arr)):
        return best_score,best_seq
    arr=np.clip(arr,0.0,1000.0)
    s=float(arr.sum())
    if s <= 0.01:
        return best_score,best_seq
    arr_norm=arr/s
    sc=score_internal(arr_norm)
    if sc < best_score:
       if verbose: print(...)
       return sc, arr_norm.copy()
    return best_score,best_seq

Let's craft final answer in code block.
</think>

```python
"""
Search for non-negative step heights minimizing

    2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / sum(a)^2

The implementation combines:

1. High-quality seed sequences, including the supplied previous best when
   available and algebraic sequences from the binomial/sqrt(1-z) construction.

2. A conv-spectral flattening iteration:
   - from the current convolution c, build a compressed target convolution
     whose dynamic range is slightly smaller;
   - take the spectral square root of the target (using X^2 = C in the
     Fourier domain, not |X|^2, since the objective is self-convolution);
   - reshape/reproject to the non-negative simplex.

3. A direct coordinate polish on the original objective, updating the
   convolution in O(n) per coordinate and optimizing small geometric
   rescalings of coordinates.

4. Random spectral/time perturbations and multi-start retrying to escape
   local minima.

The program spends the full time budget and returns the best sequence found.
"""

import time
import math
import numpy as np

try:
    from scipy.fft import fft, ifft
except Exception:
    from numpy.fft import fft, ifft


def next_fft_len(length: int) -> int:
    """Smallest power of two >= length."""
    if length <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (int(length) - 1).bit_length()


def project_simplex(v, S):
    """Project v onto {x >= 0, sum(x) = S} in Euclidean distance."""
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = v.size
    if n == 0:
        return v
    S = float(S)
    if S <= 0.0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    # Fast path: already nonnegative.
    if float(np.min(v)) >= 0.0:
        s = float(np.sum(v))
        if s <= 1e-300:
            return np.full(n, S / n, dtype=np.float64)
        return v / s

    w = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    css = np.cumsum(w)
    rho = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    theta = (css - S) / rho
    valid = theta <= w
    if np.any(valid):
        idx = int(np.max(np.where(valid)[0]))
        theta = (css[idx] - S) / rho[idx]
    else:
        theta = float(np.mean(v))

    x = np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)
    xs = float(np.sum(x))
    if xs <= 1e-300:
        x = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        x[0] = S
    else:
        x *= (S / xs)
    return x


def normalize_sum1(a):
    """Clip to nonnegative and rescale to exactly sum 1."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if a.size == 0:
        return a
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(a.size, 1.0 / max(1, a.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return a / s


def central_binomial_sequence(n):
    """
    Sequence a_k = binom(2k,k)/4^k for k=0..n-1.
    Its one-sided self-convolution is exactly 1 for lags 0..n-1.
    """
    a = np.empty(int(n), dtype=np.float64)
    if n <= 0:
        return a
    a[0] = 1.0
    for k in range(1, int(n)):
        a[k] = a[k - 1] * ((2.0 * k - 1.0) / (2.0 * k))
    return a


def stretch_sequence(a, factor=2):
    """Piecewise constant stretch by integer factor, preserving sum."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    factor = max(1, int(factor))
    out = np.zeros(a.size * factor, dtype=np.float64)
    out[0::factor] = a / float(factor)
    return out


def convolve_fft(a, n_fft=None):
    """Exact linear self-convolution of a using FFT."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = a.size
    L = 2 * n - 1
    if n_fft is None:
        n_fft = next_fft_len(L)
    A = fft(a, n_fft)
    c = np.real(ifft(A * A))
    return c[:L]


def score_internal(a, c=None):
    """Internal objective: 2*n*max(conv)/sum^2."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s <= 1e-30:
        return float("inf")
    if c is None:
        c = convolve_fft(a)
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * float(n) * m / (s * s))


def consider_candidate(arr, best_score, best_seq, tag="", verbose=True):
    """
    Normalize arr to sum 1, score it, and return improved (score, seq).
    """
    if arr is None:
        return best_score, best_seq
    arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if arr.size < 16:
        return best_score, best_seq
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(arr)):
        return best_score, best_seq

    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(np.sum(arr))
    if s <= 0.01:
        return best_score, best_seq

    arr_norm = arr / s
    sc = score_internal(arr_norm)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return best_score, best_seq

    if sc < best_score:
        if verbose:
            print("  candidate %s: score=%.6f len=%d" % (tag, sc, arr_norm.size))
        return sc, arr_norm.copy()
    return best_score, best_seq


def make_compressed_target(c, n, U):
    """
    Build a target convolution (sum 1) by linearly compressing the current
    normalized convolution v = c / mean(c) so that its maximum is about U.

    Parameters
    ----------
    c : current convolution, should have sum 1.
    n : sequence length.
    U : desired maximum in units where mean(c)=1. Must satisfy 1 < U < max(v).
    """
    n = int(n)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    mean_c = 1.0 / L
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if c.size != L:
        return None

    v = c / mean_c
    m = float(np.max(v))
    if not np.isfinite(m) or m <= 1.0 + 1e-9:
        return None
    U = float(U)
    if not np.isfinite(U) or U <= 1.0005 or U >= m - 1e-9:
        return None

    alpha = (U - 1.0) / (m - 1.0)
    if alpha <= 0.0 or alpha >= 1.0:
        return None

    target_v = alpha * v + (1.0 - alpha)
    target = target_v * mean_c
    target = np.maximum(target, 0.0)
    ts = float(np.sum(target))
    if ts <= 1e-300:
        return None
    target /= ts
    return target


def spectral_project_reference(a, target, n_fft):
    """
    Given a current sequence a (sum 1) and desired convolution target (sum 1),
    take a damped spectral square-root projection:

        sqrt(DFT(target)) -> IFFT -> project to simplex.

    Phases are aligned with the current sequence to reduce destructive branch
    switching.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = a.size
    L = 2 * n - 1
    target = np.asarray(target, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if target.size != L:
        target = target[:L]

    target_pad = np.zeros(n_fft, dtype=np.float64)
    target_pad[:L] = target

    ref_pad = np.zeros(n_fft, dtype=np.float64)
    ref_pad[:n] = a

    X_ref = fft(ref_pad)
    C = fft(target_pad)

    # Clean DC and Nyquist.
    C[0] = abs(C[0])
    nhalf = n_fft // 2
    if n_fft % 2 == 0:
        # For real output, the Nyquist root should be real.
        C[nhalf] = float(np.real(C[nhalf]))

    mag = np.sqrt(np.abs(C))
    phase = np.angle(C)
    X_target = mag * np.exp(0.5j * phase)

    # Enforce Hermitian symmetry with phase alignment to reference.
    X_target[0] = np.abs(X_target[0])
    if n_fft % 2 == 0:
        sign = 1.0
        ref_half = float(np.real(X_ref[nhalf]))
        if ref_half < 0.0:
            sign = -1.0
        X_target[nhalf] = sign * float(mag[nhalf])

        # positive half: indices 1 .. n_fft/2 - 1
        X_pos = X_target[1:nhalf].copy()
        ref_pos = X_ref[1:nhalf]
        dots = np.real(X_pos * np.conj(ref_pos))
        X_pos[dots < 0.0] = -X_pos[dots < 0.0]

        X_target[1:nhalf] = X_pos
        # negative half: N-k
        X_target[nhalf + 1:] = np.conj(X_pos[::-1])

    x_raw = np.real(ifft(X_target))[:n]
    x_raw = np.maximum(x_raw, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(x_raw))
    if s <= 1e-300:
        x_raw = a.copy()
    else:
        x_raw /= s
    return x_raw


def spectral_project_damped(a, target, n_fft, damp):
    """Damped version of spectral_project_reference in the spectrum domain."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = a.size
    L = 2 * n - 1

    target_pad = np.zeros(n_fft, dtype=np.float64)
    target_pad[:L] = target
    ref_pad = np.zeros(n_fft, dtype=np.float64)
    ref_pad[:n] = a

    X_ref = fft(ref_pad)
    C = fft(target_pad)

    C[0] = abs(C[0])
    nhalf = n_fft // 2
    if n_fft % 2 == 0:
        C[nhalf] = float(np.real(C[nhalf]))

    mag = np.sqrt(np.abs(C))
    phase = np.angle(C)
    X_target = mag * np.exp(0.5j * phase)

    X_target[0] = np.abs(X_target[0])
    if n_fft % 2 == 0:
        sign = 1.0
        if float(np.real(X_ref[nhalf])) < 0.0:
            sign = -1.0
        X_target[nhalf] = sign * float(mag[nhalf])

        X_pos = X_target[1:nhalf].copy()
        ref_pos = X_ref[1:nhalf]
        dots = np.real(X_pos * np.conj(ref_pos))
        X_pos[dots < 0.0] = -X_pos[dots < 0.0]

        X_target[1:nhalf] = X_pos
        X_target[nhalf + 1:] = np.conj(X_pos[::-1])

    X_new = (1.0 - float(damp)) * X_ref + float(damp) * X_target

    x_raw = np.real(ifft(X_new))[:n]
    x_raw = np.maximum(x_raw, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(x_raw))
    if s <= 1e-300:
        x_raw = a.copy()
    else:
        x_raw /= s
    return x_raw


def smooth_gradient_step(a, c, n_fft, tau, eta):
    """
    One projected stepsized subgradient step for a smoothed max convolution
    objective on the simplex.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = a.size
    L = 2 * n - 1
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if c.size != L:
        return a

    cur = float(np.max(c))
    if cur <= 0.0:
        return a

    T = max(1e-30, tau * cur)
    u = (c - cur) / T
    w = np.exp(u)
    ws = float(np.sum(w))
    if ws <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(ws):
        return a
    w /= ws

    w_pad = np.zeros(n_fft, dtype=np.float64)
    w_pad[:L] = w
    a_pad = np.zeros(n_fft, dtype=np.float64)
    a_pad[:n] = a

    grad = np.real(ifft(fft(w_pad) * fft(a_pad)))[:n]
    grad *= 2.0
    grad -= float(np.mean(grad))

    gn = float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
    if gn <= 1e-15:
        return a

    x = a - (eta / gn) * grad
    return project_simplex(x, 1.0)


def spectral_compress(a, n, n_fft, budget, rng, label="", verbose=True):
    """
    Iterate compressed-target spectral projections for a fixed length n.
    """
    a = normalize_sum1(np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy())
    n = int(n)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + float(budget)

    c = convolve_fft(a, n_fft)
    cur = score_internal(a, c=c)
    best = a.copy()
    best_score = cur

    if verbose:
        print("[%s] start score=%.6f n=%d" % (label, best_score, n))

    it = 0
    it_no_improve = 0
    last_print_score = best_score

    damps = np.array([0.25, 0.4, 0.6, 0.8, 1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    fix_targets = [1.5005, 1.495, 1.485, 1.47, 1.45, 1.40, 1.35]

    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 1.0:
        it += 1
        c = convolve_fft(a, n_fft)
        cur = score_internal(a, c=c)
        if not np.isfinite(cur):
            a = best.copy()
            c = convolve_fft(a, n_fft)
            cur = score_internal(a, c=c)
            continue

        if cur < best_score:
            best_score = cur
            best = a.copy()

        if verbose and (it % 250 == 0 or best_score <= last_print_score - 5e-4):
            print("[%s] it=%d score=%.6f best=%.6f" % (label, it, cur, best_score))
            last_print_score = best_score

        m = float(np.max(c))
        if m <= 0.0:
            break

        v_max = m / (1.0 / L)

        # Construct candidate target maxima.
        pool = []
        eros = [0.0005, 0.001, 0.002, 0.005, 0.01, 0.02, 0.05, 0.09]
        for er in eros:
            u = v_max * (1.0 - er)
            if u > 1.0005 and u < v_max - 1e-9:
                pool.append(u)
        for ufix in fix_targets:
            if 1.0005 < ufix < v_max - 1e-9:
                pool.append(ufix)

        if not pool:
            pool = [v_max * 0.999]

        if it_no_improve > 80:
            # Try a bigger jump.
            u_idx = int(rng.integers(len(pool)))
        else:
            # Mild when close, broader when far/stalled.
            weights = np.ones(len(pool), dtype=np.float64)
            if best_score < 1.52:
                for i, u in enumerate(pool):
                    if v_max - u < 0.02:
                        weights[i] *= 5.0
            u_idx = int(rng.choice(len(pool), p=weights / weights.sum()))

        U = float(pool[u_idx])
        target = make_compressed_target(c, n, U)
        if target is None:
            # Small perturbation to move.
            sigma = 1e-4 / math.sqrt(max(1, n))
            a = project_simplex(a + rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n), 1.0)
            continue

        damp = float(rng.choice(damps))
        if it_no_improve > 150:
            damp = max(0.8, damp)
        else:
            damp = min(0.9, damp)

        a_new = spectral_project_damped(a, target, n_fft, damp)
        sc_new = score_internal(a_new)
        if np.isfinite(sc_new) and sc_new < cur:
            a = a_new
            it_no_improve = 0
        else:
            # Try a milder near-current target.
            U_mild = v_max * (1.0 - 0.001)
            target_mild = make_compressed_target(c, n, U_mild)
            if target_mild is not None:
                a_mild = spectral_project_damped(a, target_mild, n_fft, 0.35)
                sc_mild = score_internal(a_mild)
                if np.isfinite(sc_mild) and sc_mild < cur:
                    a = a_mild
                    it_no_improve = 0
                else:
                    it_no_improve += 1
            else:
                it_no_improve += 1

        # Occasionally use gradient subproblem.
        if it_no_improve > 30 and it % 4 == 0:
            a_grad = smooth_gradient_step(a, c, n_fft, 0.15, 0.02)
            sc_grad = score_internal(a_grad)
            if np.isfinite(sc_grad) and sc_grad < cur:
                a = a_grad
                it_no_improve = 0

        # If stuck, perturb from best.
        if it_no_improve > 120:
            sigmas = np.array([2e-4, 5e-4, 1e-3, 2e-3], dtype=np.float64) / math.sqrt(max(1, n))
            sigma = float(rng.choice(sigmas))
            a = project_simplex(best + rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n), 1.0)
            it_no_improve = 0

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 0.5:
        sc_last = score_internal(a)
        if np.isfinite(sc_last) and sc_last < best_score:
            best_score = sc_last
            best = a.copy()

    if verbose:
        print("[%s] done score=%.6f" % (label, best_score))
    return best, best_score


def coordinate_polish(a, n, n_fft, budget, rng, label="", verbose=True):
    """
    Coordinate descent directly on the score.  For each coordinate, try several
    geometric rescalings and use the exact O(n) update of affected convolution
    lags.
    """
    a = normalize_sum1(np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy())
    n = int(n)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + float(budget)

    c = convolve_fft(a, n_fft)
    cur = score_internal(a, c=c)
    best = a.copy()
    best_score = cur

    if verbose:
        print("[%s] coordinate start score=%.6f n=%d" % (label, best_score, n))

    factors = np.array(
        [0.10, 0.25, 0.40, 0.60, 0.75, 0.90, 1.10, 1.25, 1.50, 2.00],
        dtype=np.float64,
    )

    top_lag_count = min(L, 28)
    var_count = max(24, min(n, 72))
    rand_count = max(12, min(n // 10, var_count // 3))

    rounds = 0
    no_improve_rounds = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 1.0:
        rounds += 1

        # Force exactness periodically.
        if rounds % 5 == 1:
            c = convolve_fft(a, n_fft)
        cur = score_internal(a, c=c)
        if not np.isfinite(cur):
            a = best.copy()
            c = convolve_fft(a, n_fft)
            cur = score_internal(a, c=c)

        if cur < best_score:
            best_score = cur
            best = a.copy()

        if verbose and (rounds % 25 == 0 or best_score <= 1.505):
            print("[%s] coord round=%d score=%.6f best=%.6f" % (label, rounds, cur, best_score))

        # Choose active lags.
        if top_lag_count >= L:
            top_lags = np.arange(L, dtype=np.int64)
        else:
            top_lags = np.argpartition(c, -top_lag_count)[-top_lag_count:]

        contrib = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for k64 in top_lags:
            k = int(k64)
            low = max(0, k - n + 1)
            high = min(k, n - 1)
            if low > high:
                continue
            ii = np.arange(low, high + 1, dtype=np.int64)
            jj = k - ii
            contrib[ii] += 2.0 * a[ii] * a[jj]

        if var_count >= n:
            top_vars = np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64)
        else:
            top_vars = np.argpartition(contrib, -var_count)[-var_count:]
        rand_vars = rng.integers(0, n, size=rand_count, dtype=np.int64)
        all_vars = np.unique(np.concatenate((top_vars, rand_vars)))
        max_vars = min(len(all_vars), n, 96)
        all_vars = all_vars[:max_vars]

        old_sum = float(np.sum(a))
        if old_sum <= 1e-300:
            break

        improved_round = False
        for i64 in all_vars:
            i = int(i64)
            old = float(a[i])
            if old <= 1e-15:
                continue

            slice_len = min(n, 2 * n - 1 - i)
            if slice_len <= 0:
                continue
            right = i + slice_len

            # Outside max.
            if i > 0:
                out_left = float(np.max(c[:i]))
            else:
                out_left = 0.0
            if right < L:
                out_right = float(np.max(c[right:]))
            else:
                out_right = 0.0
            out_max = max(out_left, out_right)

            old_vec = a[:slice_len].copy()
            deltas = old * (factors - 1.0)
            Ss = old_sum + deltas
            bad = Ss <= 1e-12
            if np.any(bad):
                Ss[bad] = 1e-12

            base = c[i:right]
            cand = base[None, :] + (2.0 * deltas[:, None] * old_vec[None, :])
            maxes = np.maximum(np.max(cand, axis=1), out_max)
            scores = (2.0 * float(n) * maxes) / (Ss * Ss)
            idx = int(np.argmin(scores))

            if np.isfinite(scores[idx]) and scores[idx] < cur - 1e-13:
                delta = float(deltas[idx])
                S_new = float(Ss[idx])
                if S_new <= 1e-12:
                    continue

                # Update convolution exactly for raw new sequence.
                c[i:right] += 2.0 * delta * old_vec
                a[i] = old + delta

                # Renormalize to sum 1.
                scale = 1.0 / S_new
                a *= scale
                c *= (scale * scale)

                cur = score_internal(a, c=c)
                improved_round = True

                if cur < best_score:
                    best_score = cur
                    best = a.copy()

        if not improved_round:
            no_improve_rounds += 1
            # Random local kick.
            if no_improve_rounds % 3 == 0 and len(all_vars) > 0:
                kick_vars = all_vars[:min(12, len(all_vars))]
                for vx in kick_vars:
                    vx = int(vx)
                    if a[vx] > 0.0:
                        a[vx] *= float(rng.uniform(0.35, 1.8))
                a = normalize_sum1(a)
                c = convolve_fft(a, n_fft)
                cur = score_internal(a, c=c)
                if cur < best_score:
                    best_score = cur
                    best = a.copy()
                    no_improve_rounds = 0
            else:
                # Larger perturbation from best.
                sigma = float(rng.uniform(1e-4, 2e-3)) / math.sqrt(max(1, n))
                a = project_simplex(best + rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n), 1.0)
                c = convolve_fft(a, n_fft)
                cur = score_internal(a, c=c)
                if cur < best_score:
                    best_score = cur
                    best = a.copy()
                no_improve_rounds = 0

        else:
            no_improve_rounds = 0

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 0.5:
        sc_last = score_internal(a)
        if np.isfinite(sc_last) and sc_last < best_score:
            best_score = sc_last
            best = a.copy()

    if verbose:
        print("[%s] coordinate done score=%.6f" % (label, best_score))
    return best, best_score


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """
    Entry point required by the evaluation harness.
    Returns a Python list of nonnegative floats.
    """
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) if seed is not None else 42)
    budget_s = float(budget_s) if budget_s is not None else 2400.0
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    # Leave a safety margin for cleanup/evaluation.
    hard_deadline = t0 + max(60.0, budget_s - 45.0)

    best_score = float("inf")
    best_seq = None

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Seed from previous known state-of-the-art, if available.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    raw_prev = None
    prev_norm = None
    hseq = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if hseq is not None:
        try:
            raw_prev = np.asarray(hseq, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
            if raw_prev.size >= 16 and np.all(np.isfinite(raw_prev)):
                raw_prev = np.clip(raw_prev, 0.0, 1000.0)
                s = float(np.sum(raw_prev))
                if s > 0.01:
                    prev_norm = raw_prev / s
        except Exception:
            raw_prev = None
            prev_norm = None

    if raw_prev is not None and prev_norm is not None:
        best_score, best_seq = consider_candidate(prev_norm, best_score, best_seq, "previous", verbose=True)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Choose main optimization length.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    n_main = 8192
    if prev_norm is not None and 4096 <= len(prev_norm) <= 16384:
        n_main = int(len(prev_norm))
    elif best_seq is not None and 4096 <= len(best_seq) <= 16384:
        n_main = int(len(best_seq))

    N_main = next_fft_len(2 * n_main - 1)

    # Algebraic seed for main length.
    central = central_binomial_sequence(n_main)
    best_score_c, seq_c = consider_candidate(central, best_score, best_seq, "central", verbose=True)
    if len(seq_c) == n_main and (seq_c.size == n_main):
        # We will separately keep a primary sequence of length n_main.
        pass

    if best_seq is None or best_seq.size != n_main:
        primary_seq = normalize_sum1(central)
    else:
        primary_seq = best_seq.copy()

    primary_score = score_internal(primary_seq)
    rem = hard_deadline - time.monotonic()

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Phase 1: deep spectral compression on the primary length.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    if rem > 80.0:
        phase1_budget = min(1350.0, max(80.0, rem * 0.50))
        seq1, score1 = spectral_compress(
            primary_seq,
            n_main,
            N_main,
            phase1_budget,
            rng,
            label="S1",
            verbose=True,
        )
        if score1 < primary_score:
            primary_score = score1
            primary_seq = seq1
        best_score, best_seq = consider_candidate(seq1, best_score, best_seq, "S1", verbose=True)
        if best_seq is not None and best_seq.size == n_main and best_score < primary_score:
            primary_score = best_score
            primary_seq = best_seq.copy()

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Phase 2: alternative start / perturbation, same length.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    rem = hard_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 120.0:
        phase2_budget = min(600.0, max(60.0, rem * 0.30))
        if primary_score < 1.55:
            start2 = project_simplex(
                primary_seq + rng.normal(0.0, 3e-4 / math.sqrt(max(1, n_main)), n_main),
                1.0,
            )
        else:
            start2 = normalize_sum1(central_binomial_sequence(n_main))
        seq2, score2 = spectral_compress(
            start2,
            n_main,
            N_main,
            phase2_budget,
            rng,
            label="S2",
            verbose=True,
        )
        if score2 < primary_score:
            primary_score = score2
            primary_seq = seq2
        best_score, best_seq = consider_candidate(seq2, best_score, best_seq, "S2", verbose=True)
        if best_seq is not None and best_seq.size == n_main and best_score < primary_score:
            primary_score = best_score
            primary_seq = best_seq.copy()

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Phase 3: coordinate polish on primary.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    rem = hard_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 60.0:
        coord_budget = min(900.0, max(60.0, rem * 0.55))
        seq3, score3 = coordinate_polish(
            primary_seq,
            n_main,
            N_main,
            coord_budget,
            rng,
            label="C1",
            verbose=True,
        )
        if score3 < primary_score:
            primary_score = score3
            primary_seq = seq3
        best_score, best_seq = consider_candidate(seq3, best_score, best_seq, "C1", verbose=True)
        if best_seq is not None and best_seq.size == n_main and best_score < primary_score:
            primary_score = best_score
            primary_seq = best_seq.copy()

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Phase 4: short spectral fine-tuning if time remains.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    rem = hard_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 50.0 and primary_score > 1.30:
        phase4_budget = min(180.0, max(30.0, rem * 0.40))
        seq4, score4 = spectral_compress(
            primary_seq,
            n_main,
            N_main,
            phase4_budget,
            rng,
            label="S3",
            verbose=True,
        )
        if score4 < primary_score:
            primary_score = score4
            primary_seq = seq4
        best_score, best_seq = consider_candidate(seq4, best_score, best_seq, "S3", verbose=True)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Phase 5: try a stretched 2x length seed/sequence from previous if
    # available.  Larger length can sometimes improve the asymptotic constant.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    rem = hard_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 240.0 and raw_prev is not None and len(raw_prev) == 8192:
        stretched_raw = stretch_sequence(raw_prev / float(np.sum(raw_prev)), factor=2)
        stretched = normalize_sum1(stretched_raw)
        stretched, stretched_score_c = consider_candidate(
            stretched, best_score, best_seq, "stretch", verbose=True
        )
        if stretched is not None and stretched.size >= 4096 and best_score > 1.5027:
            n_st = int(stretched.size)
            N_st = next_fft_len(2 * n_st - 1)
            st_budget = min(260.0, max(45.0, rem * 0.25))
            seq_st, score_st = coordinate_polish(
                stretched,
                n_st,
                N_st,
                st_budget,
                rng,
                label="/stretch",
                verbose=True,
            )
            best_score, best_seq = consider_candidate(seq_st, best_score, best_seq, "stretchC", verbose=True)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Final small cleanup: coordinate on global best if length is modest.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    rem = hard_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 45.0 and best_seq is not None and best_seq.size <= 32768:
        n_final = int(best_seq.size)
        N_final = next_fft_len(2 * n_final - 1)
        final_budget = min(180.0, max(20.0, rem * 0.80))
        seqF, scoreF = coordinate_polish(
            best_seq,
            n_final,
            N_final,
            final_budget,
            rng,
            label="Cfinal",
            verbose=True,
        )
        best_score, best_seq = consider_candidate(seqF, best_score, best_seq, "Cfinal", verbose=True)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Prepare final sequence and verify.
    # Prefer normalized, clip, and keep finite.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    if best_seq is None:
        best_seq = normalize_sum1(central_binomial_sequence(8192))

    final_arr = np.asarray(best_seq, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    final_arr = np.clip(final_arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    fs = float(np.sum(final_arr))
    if not np.isfinite(fs) or fs <= 0.01:
        final_arr = normalize_sum1(central_binomial_sequence(256))

    # If the evaluated sequence is invalid, fall back to previous or central.
    final_list = [float(x) for x in final_arr]
    final_eval = float("inf")
    try:
        final_eval = float(evaluate_sequence(final_list))
    except Exception:
        final_eval = float("inf")

    if not np.isfinite(final_eval):
        if prev_norm is not None:
            final_list = [float(x) for x in np.clip(prev_norm, 0.0, 1000.0)]
        else:
            final_list = [float(x) for x in normalize_sum1(central_binomial_sequence(256))]

    print("best internal score=%.6f final len=%d" % (best_score, len(final_list)))
    return final_list
```
